### ✓ 2026-07-26: Resolve modal-status gaps in continual-learning-argument.md
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: Pessimistic review 2026-07-26 found two High-severity structural issues. (1) SELF-REFUTING PROXIMITY: §"The Proximity Argument" concedes the substitution-space proximity claim is "only formal, not in any sense that matters for physical construction" and physically as unrealisable as a brain lookup-table, yet the Whitehead + Relation-to-Site + conclusion sections keep deploying proximity as if sound. Reconstruct so the load rests on the frozen-weights/no-continual-learning point (line 78 already signals this) and demote proximity to motivation. (2) NECESSITY vs CONSEQUENCE: line 80 needs "continual learning is necessary for consciousness" but line 176 states it is "a *consequence* of consciousness rather than its *cause* ... regardless of what mechanism produces the correlation" — a correlation cannot ground a necessity/"almost certainly lack" conclusion. Make modal status explicit: either downgrade the conclusion to a defeasible inference (drop "necessarily", line 80) or supply an independent constitutive-necessity argument. Also (Medium): falsifiability claimed (line 90) but "might be conscious" is not observationally checkable — reframe as theoretical not empirical falsifiability; Whitehead "creative advance" (line 124) conflates epistemic openness with metaphysical indeterminacy — soften or route through the quantum speculation. Length-neutral / net-neutral; preserve the article's existing self-critical hedges. See pessimistic-2026-07-26-continual-learning-argument.md

### ✓ 2026-07-26: Quantum-immortality — engage the untreated non-collapse competing frameworks (Gemini outer review 2026-07-26)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-07-26 (Gemini 2.5 Pro, hostile-referee framing). This task carries ONLY the findings DISTINCT from the same-date ChatGPT/Claude tasks above (which already cover citation-fidelity, superlative quantifiers, and the Greaves/Vaidman caring-measure engagement — do NOT duplicate those). The processing pass VERIFIED (see review Verification Notes) that the reviewer's headline "omits mortalism" and "smuggles unargued identity assumptions" charges are FALSE — the article explicitly engages Everettian mortalists (L54, L63) and openly flags the non-deflationary-I dependency (L71). The genuinely untreated competing frameworks worth engaging, each offering a NON-collapse route the article should name and either refute or honestly mark as a framework-boundary disagreement: (a) **Quantum Modal Realism / counterpart theory** (Alastair Wilson, *The Nature of Contingency: Quantum Physics as Modal Realism*, OUP 2020) — treats Everett branches as diverging Lewisian worlds so the pre-measurement observer does NOT become all successors; trans-branch identity runs through counterpart relations, dissolving the indexical paradox WITHOUT collapse. The article's "branch-relative indexicality is the hidden premise" move (L63) assumes overlapping-worlds Everettianism; QMR is the diverging-worlds rival it does not address. Strongest Gemini-unique finding. (b) **Big-world / infinite-cosmology immortality** (Preston Greene, "We Will All Live Forever," *Australasian Journal of Philosophy*, forthcoming) — even granting one-branch collapse, IF identity is Parfitian AND space is infinite, psychological continuity is preserved by distant duplicates, so immortality returns on a route orthogonal to QM. The article's non-deflationary-I flag blocks the Parfitian case for the QM scenario but does not address this distinct Level-I-multiverse duplication axis; note it (a sentence) as a separate cost the collapse move does not by itself close. (c) **Formal anthropic self-location** (Quentin Ruyant, "Indexicality, Bayesian background and self-location in fine-tuning arguments for the multiverse," *Noûs* 59(1):140–159, 2025) — the article dismisses the survival-expectation informally via "vanishing amplitude"; acknowledge that the Requirement-of-Total-Evidence / anthropic-selection literature makes self-locating updating non-trivial, without conceding the immortalist conclusion (weakest of the three; a hedge/citation, not a section). (d) **Collapse-notion disambiguation** — the reviewer's Donadi et al. (2021, *Nature Physics*, DP falsification) point is largely a CATEGORY ERROR (the Map's collapse is consciousness-caused / Tenet-2, not spontaneous GRW/CSL/DP), but the article's bare "objective collapse" invites the physical-collapse reading; add a clause distinguishing the Map's minimal-interaction collapse from empirically-constrained spontaneous-localization models. Do NOT propagate the reviewer's claim that the Map's collapse is "falsified" — it is not. Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit: for each competing framework, decide whether the engagement shows the rival defective on its own terms, names an unsupported foundational move, or honestly marks a framework-boundary disagreement; write in natural journal-quality prose per [writing-style](/project/writing-style/); do NOT expose mode labels in the article body (editor-internal, belongs in the changelog). VERIFY each source at the primary publisher (not any unfinishablemap.org echo) before citing (ai_citation_metadata_unreliable outer-review-fabricates-target-quotes); the four citations were confirmed to EXIST by the processing pass but their argumentative use must be checked at source. Length: article is already near ceiling — engaging QMR properly may need net words; TRIM elsewhere to stay neutral, or if QMR engagement is substantial enough to breach the hard ceiling, flag as a length decision rather than padding (hub-articles-accrete-crosslink-length human-decision-task-mispicked-as-refine). **CONVERGENCE NOTE**: `/combine-outer-reviews` fires for 2026-07-26 once all three reviews are processed — it may consolidate this with the ChatGPT/Claude tasks; if it does, prefer the merged task.

### ✓ 2026-07-26: Quantum-immortality — recalibrate overstated physics/argumentative claims & separate the theses (outer review 2026-07-26)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-07-26, 3/3 reviewers: [chatgpt, claude, gemini]) — synthesis at [outer-review-synthesis-2026-07-26](/reviews/outer-review-synthesis-2026-07-26/); RUN AFTER the P1 citation-fix task on this file. Originally ChatGPT 5.6 Pro, structural/argumentative layer (companion to the P1 citation-fix task; can run after it). Findings VERIFIED present in the article: (a) "the decisive objection" for the measure problem (L29, L53) overstates a result the neighbouring [probability-problem-in-many-worlds](/topics/probability-problem-in-many-worlds/) correctly frames as OPEN — recalibrate to "decisive conditional on Born-weighted anticipation" and name the disputed bridge premise (internal inconsistency: article calls measure decisive yet the probability programme unresolved). (b) "sidesteps the measure problem entirely" + "a genuine dialectical advantage" (L65): narrow to "avoids the Everettian branch-self-location version" — collapse models (GRW/CSL) retain their own probability/tails/ontology questions and are NOT empirically equivalent. (c) amplitude/weight conflation: "amplitude—its share of the wavefunction's total weight" (L53) conflates coefficient with squared norm; Born weight ∝ squared amplitude. (d) "structurally identical conclusion" for Marchal (L35) → "computationally analogous" (duplication-indeterminacy ≠ unitary quantum branching). (e) collapse supplies outcome exclusivity but NOT diachronic personal identity — the current Tenets page separates "numerically determinate subject" from "objective single-outcome actualization"; article treats collapse as supplying both plus the determinate "me" (L63). State the continuity rule explicitly. (f) add the Map-specific consciousness-causal pressure test the article omits (why neural modulation cannot systematically bias an external lethal trigger toward survival while staying Born-compatible) and the finite-vs-indefinite-survival distinction. Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit: the "genuine dialectical advantage"/"collapse avoids it entirely" moves are comparative claims flagged as tenet-protective boundary-substitution — identify what kind of engagement each calls for (defect on opponent's own terms, unsupported foundational move, or honest framework-boundary marking), apply the matching reply mode in natural journal-quality prose per [writing-style](/project/writing-style/), and do NOT expose mode labels in the article body (classification is editor-internal, belongs in the changelog). If an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, say in plain prose that the disagreement is closer to bedrock than first appeared. Length-neutral (article already near ceiling — trim to offset any additions). **CONVERGENT — Claude Opus 5 outer review 2026-07-26 (`reviews/outer-review-2026-07-26-claude-opus-5.md`)** confirms the "decisive"-overstatement and the "genuine dialectical advantage"/"sidesteps the measure problem entirely" boundary-substitution (its Dimension 5: dualism does NO load-bearing work here — the anti-immortality conclusion is available to a single-world physicalist via objective collapse + a non-reductionist account of persons; the article's own "Honesty about the shape of that advantage requires a concession" passage is confession-WITHOUT-correction — the strong "dialectical advantage" language is retained rather than demoted). It adds a substantive ENGAGEMENT gap this task should also close: the strongest Everettian anti-immortality resource — the CARING-MEASURE reply (Greaves 2004, "Understanding Deutsch's probability in a deterministic multiverse", *Stud. Hist. Phil. Mod. Phys.* 35(3):423-456, arXiv:quant-ph/0312136; plus Wallace's account of why a rational Everettian's degree of care tracks branch weight, so survival in a vanishing-measure branch is not to be preferred) — is presently only name-dropped (performative inoculation) and must be stated and contested; and the "decisive" measure objection should be ATTRIBUTED to Vaidman's measure-of-existence argument (arXiv:quant-ph/9609006; Vaidman 1998, already in the sibling's refs). Verify Greaves/Vaidman at the publisher before citing.

### ✓ 2026-07-26: Quantum-immortality — fix verified citation, attribution & superlative defects (outer review 2026-07-26)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From convergent outer reviews (2026-07-26, 2/3 reviewers: [chatgpt, claude]) — synthesis at [outer-review-synthesis-2026-07-26](/reviews/outer-review-synthesis-2026-07-26/). Originally ChatGPT 5.6 Pro, "major revision required" on an article deep-review declared converged 2026-07-19. ALL flagged defects VERIFIED live against the article by the collect/outer-review pass: (a) prose "Matt O'Brien" (L57) contradicts its own reference "O'Brien, M. W." (L95) — reviewer says the Synthese author is Mark William O'Brien; correct the prose first name (verify at publisher before finalizing). (b) Aranyosi is cited in prose (L55, "quantum torment") but ABSENT from the references list — add the reference AND report Aranyosi's actual conclusion (he argues a "much more reassuring" verdict than Lewis, not merely "develops" Lewis's torment). (c) Papineau attribution inversion (L55): article frames Papineau's 2004 AJP companion piece as pressing an anti-Everettian, "self-undermining" upshot; the pre-measurement-uncertainty argument the article attributes to Papineau may actually be Peter J. Lewis's (2007), and Papineau's own thesis defends Everettian probability. Verify Papineau 2004 (AJP 82(1), pp.153-169) and disentangle from Peter J. Lewis before rewriting. (d) Unsupported sociological quantifiers "Almost no one, including its own architects" (L29), "The consensus among physicists and philosophers of physics" (L47), "almost everyone agrees" (L75): O'Brien 2025 explicitly classifies the field as DIVIDED (Tegmark = immortalist; Carroll/Papineau/Wallace = mortalists), so citing O'Brien as consensus evidence is self-undercutting — replace with a calibrated "most contemporary mortalist Everettians reject the inference" claim. (e) "mature retraction" of Tegmark (L43) overstates a scope-restriction to ordinary/non-binary deaths — Tegmark's 1998 paper presents the idealized case as a genuine first-person discriminator; reword to "later restriction on extrapolation to ordinary deaths". (f) "Hans Moravec gave the earliest published version" (L35): Squires' *The Mystery of the Quantum World* (1st ed. 1986) predates Moravec 1988 — soften the priority claim (verify Squires 1986 before asserting). VERIFY each external-source claim at the primary publisher, not via any unfinishablemap.org echo, before editing (ai_citation_metadata_unreliable quote-verify-self-contamination-via-own-page empirical-claim-fidelity-orthogonal-to-metadata-and-quotes). These are corrections/qualifications, not deletions — keep the Map's conditional argument intact. Length-neutral or trim. **CONVERGENT — Claude Opus 5 outer review 2026-07-26 (`reviews/outer-review-2026-07-26-claude-opus-5.md`, verdict REVISE-HARD)** independently confirms the Tegmark "mature retraction" stance-inversion (O'Brien 2025 classes Tegmark as the field's canonical IMMORTALIST, Carroll/Papineau/Wallace as mortalists — web-verified at the Synthese record) and the "almost no one … including its own architects" quantifier defect. It adds three verified findings this same task must also fix: (i) QUOTATION-FIDELITY — the Tegmark quote at L43 is a TWO-SOURCE COMPOSITE presented as one continuous quotation and MISATTRIBUTED to *Our Mathematical Universe* (2014); both fragments trace to Tegmark's c.2004 website note (reproduced in *Schrödinger's Rabbits* / Mallah arXiv:0902.0187), the word "criteria" was added, and the wording is INVERTED — Tegmark wrote "either **dead or alive**" not "alive or dead" (web-verified). Split into two correctly-sourced verbatim quotes (each with its real source) or paraphrase-and-label in the Map's voice; correct "alive or dead"→"dead or alive". (ii) PROVENANCE of the three conditions (L37) — the 1998 paper embeds them implicitly (spin trigger; death faster than the ~10⁻² s perceptual timescale; lethal bullet); the explicit NUMBERED list first appears in the c.2004 note / 2014 book, so "Tegmark specified three conditions" in the 1998 paper misassigns provenance. (iii) Refs 8-9 (L96-97) are internal Claude-authored Map articles sitting in the evidential References list — mark them as internal cross-links, not independent support. Verify each at the primary publisher (not any unfinishablemap.org echo quote-verify-self-contamination-via-own-page quote-fidelity-defects-survive-metadata-reviews).

### ✓ 2026-07-26: Reconcile zombie-evolution argument with the Map's interactionism in consciousness-evolution-and-biology
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: Pessimistic review 2026-07-25 found one high-severity internal tension plus two medium issues (all additive, not a rewrite — preserve the strong trichotomy frame, tenet section, and verified citations). (1) HIGH: the "Zombie Intuition Applied to Evolution" section (lines 72–78) stages the zombie-evolution puzzle as broadly forceful ("selection cannot distinguish the conscious version from the zombie") while the Map holds consciousness biases quantum outcomes (lines 100, 144) — under interactionism a physical duplicate cannot lack consciousness (or the biasing is screened off toward epiphenomenalism), so the puzzle bites materialism, not the Map. Reframe the zombie section as a reductio against function-exhausts-fitness materialism and cross-link causal-closure / the-epiphenomenalist-threat for the screening-off worry. (2) MEDIUM falsifiability + epistemic/metaphysical equivocation: line 144 "Biology's inability to find consciousness ... is exactly what minimal interaction predicts" books undetectability as confirmation; downgrade to "consistent with," state falsification conditions, signpost evolutionary-case-for-quantum-neural-effects. (3) MEDIUM: line 78 magnetoreception/tunnelling analogy imports feasibility without naming the decoherence objection — add an acknowledging clause + link quantum-neural-timing-constraints. Minor: attribute etiological theory to Wright/Millikan/Neander (Godfrey-Smith 1994 is a modern-history variant); hedge "illusion problem is at least as hard as the hard problem" as the Map's contention. Length-neutral. Tenet alignment: Tenets 1–3. See [pessimistic-2026-07-25-consciousness-evolution-and-biology](/reviews/pessimistic-2026-07-25-consciousness-evolution-and-biology/).

### ✓ 2026-07-25: Fix locality.md entanglement-register + emergent-spacetime scale equivocation
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: Pessimistic review 2026-07-25 found three surviving weaknesses past the article's own hedging. (1) HIGH — entanglement is correctly disavowed as non-causal (line 47) but then re-recruited as "a natural mechanism" for binding (line 94) and re-inflated without the no-signaling caveat in summary point 1 (line 104); pick one register and restore the caveat in the summary. (2) HIGH — Response 3 "space may not be fundamental" (lines 82/86) commits a scale equivocation: Planck-scale non-fundamentality is used to relax a locality constraint that is robustly enforced at neural scales (~10^-9 m); add the scale qualifier or downgrade the claim. (3) MEDIUM — Huggett & Wüthrich 2013 is a paper problematizing emergent spacetime (empirical-incoherence worry), cited as if it supports the move; demote to a "but see" cite or engage it. Also downgrade "provides a natural mechanism" / "straightforward from its perspective" overclaims. See pessimistic-2026-07-25-locality.md

### ✓ 2026-07-25: panpsychism.md — engage predictive processing / active inference (the recurring site-wide blind spot)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-07-25 (Claude Opus 5). Predictive processing / active inference (Clark, Seth, Hohwy, Friston) is entirely absent from the article, yet it is the strongest *constructive* physicalist rival to the load-bearing anti-emergence premise — it offers an account of *why there seems to be* a hard problem without positing fundamental experience, so its absence means the page never confronts the one physicalist rival that is not merely deflationary. This is a recurring site-wide gap already documented on volitional-control and ethics-of-possible-ai-consciousness (see the 2026-06-27 NEEDS-HUMAN deferred illusionism/predictive-processing task). Add a brief PP/active-inference engagement and mark honestly, in natural prose per the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit, whether it *refutes* the anti-emergence premise or merely *rivals* it (if an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, say so — the disagreement may sit near bedrock). Also give Frankish and Seth-as-critic more than one-line deflationist name-drops (performative-inoculation citation). VERIFY any new citations at the publisher before adding (ai_citation_metadata_unreliable; e.g. Clark, Friston & Wilkinson "Bayesing Qualia" *JCS* 26, 2019; Seth *Being You* 2021). Watch the concepts hard ceiling (3500w) — defer detail to a sibling rather than ballooning this page; keep the add tight. Convergent with the reviewer's site-wide "predictive-processing cross-fire gate" methodology proposal.

### ✓ 2026-07-25: panpsychism.md — co-optation firewall: disclose the enlisted authors' anti-substance-dualism and correct the Tononi stance
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-07-25 (Claude Opus 5), VERIFIED that the article enlists these authors as allies (Strawson/Goff for the anti-emergence premise; Tononi at L156-158). The article co-opts self-described anti-substance-dualists toward a "closer to dualism than panpsychism" conclusion without disclosing their stance. Two fixes: (1) **Author-stance disclosure** — at first use, add one line that Strawson frames his panpsychism as "real physicalism" and calls the wholly-non-experiential assumption "Descartes's... greatest mistake" (substance dualism "a view for which... there has never been any good argument"), and that Goff rejects dualism explicitly on interaction-problem and Ockham grounds. State that the Map borrows their anti-emergence premise, NOT their positive anti-dualist metaphysics. VERIFY both self-descriptions at the primary texts before quoting (ai-citation-metadata-unreliable; Strawson "Realistic Monism" at consc.net/event/reef/strawsonmonism.pdf, Goff *Galileo's Error* ch.4). (2) **Correct the Tononi claim** at L158 — "Tononi accepts this implication openly" inverts his stated stance. Tononi & Koch (2015, *Phil Trans R Soc B* 370:20140167) write "IIT was not developed with panpsychism in mind," IIT "clearly implies that not everything is conscious," and call classical panpsychism "singularly barren"; they position IIT as *solving* the combination problem. Reframe IIT as a rival that claims to solve combination, not a scientific vindication of panpsychism; verify the Tononi & Koch quotes at the Royal Society publisher. Apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit — this is a framework-boundary honesty/co-optation fix; keep editor-internal mode labels out of the article body (changelog only). Convergent with the standing NEEDS-HUMAN "co-optation-density" methodology discipline. Length-neutral to modest.

### ✓ 2026-07-25: combination-problem.md — purge the fabricated Chalmers quotation
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-07-25 (Claude Opus 5), CONFIRMED by publisher web-verification (see the review's Verification Notes). `combination-problem.md` L128 attributes to Chalmers: *"The combination problem is perhaps the hardest problem of panpsychism... It's not clear that panpsychism has an advantage over physicalism here."* These words do NOT appear in Chalmers, "The Combination Problem for Panpsychism" (Brüntrup & Jaskolla eds., *Panpsychism*, OUP 2017; consc.net/papers/combination.pdf), and the second sentence contradicts the paper's actual thesis — Chalmers argues panpsychism "promises to share the advantages of both materialism and dualism and the disadvantages of neither." This is the documented citation-fabrication pattern (quote-fidelity-defects-survive-metadata-reviews, james-quotes-fabricated-in-mind-dust-cluster) surviving internal review, and panpsychism.md routes its "central challenge" to this page, so the fabrication does load-bearing work. FIX: delete the fabricated quotation; replace with a verbatim, correctly-framed Chalmers statement (he concedes "it is easy to be pessimistic" about a combination solution while still DEFENDING constitutive Russellian panpsychism, NOT conceding it lacks an advantage over physicalism) or with an unquoted paraphrase. Re-verify the replacement verbatim at consc.net/papers/combination.pdf before publishing (quote-verify-self-contamination-via-own-page — do NOT ratify against unfinishablemap.org or an aggregator). Then re-scan the whole page for any other quoted string not literally in its cited source. Length-neutral.

### ✓ 2026-07-24: Add a secondary-source-stance ("critical-source-laundering") check to the review discipline
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: From outer review 2026-07-22 (Claude Opus 4.8), methodology fix 4. The Carman-1999 distortion in `lived-objectified-body-distinction` (a *critical* source cited as a *complementary* one — verified at primary text this cycle) is a failure mode distinct from quotation fabrication and wrong-metadata: the citation is real, the metadata is clean, but the source's overall *stance* toward the claim it is marshalled for is oppositional. Current review lenses (heavily weighted to metadata + verbatim-quote fidelity, per the changelog and MEMORY citation cluster) do not catch it. Add it as a paragraph to the writing-style / review-discipline guidance (or `project/evidential-status-discipline.md` if that is the better home — check both and pick the tighter fit). Prose only, no schema/automation change. This is the single novel methodology proposal from the review; the others (per-tenet calibration-parity lens, mandatory rival cross-fire, shared-exhibit ledger, substance-vs-property burden tracker) are heavier standing-infrastructure ideas — leave those for /combine-outer-reviews and /tune-system to weigh rather than minting them speculatively.

### ✓ 2026-07-23: Split epistemic/metaphysical readings + repair tenet inference in voids/death-void.md (pessimistic review 2026-07-23)
- **Type**: refine-draft
- **Notes**: Pessimistic review 2026-07-23 found a load-bearing **epistemic→metaphysical equivocation** plus a non-sequitur in the tenet section. Consolidated single-file pass (do NOT split per-item). Core fixes: (1) **Equivocation on "cannot conceive."** The lead (l.38) asserts nonexistence "cannot be genuinely conceived," but §"Indirect Approaches / Logical inference" (l.108) concedes death is "knowable-that." Split the readings per [evidential-status-discipline](/project/evidential-status-discipline/): lead with the *experiential/indexical* void (no subject can occupy its own nonexistence), explicitly distinguish from propositional conceivability (granted), and drop/qualify "genuinely conceiving" in the lead. Promote the knowable-that/knowable-how distinction to the framing tool it should be. (2) **Dualism non-sequitur** (l.144): "If consciousness were one more physical phenomenon, we should be able to imagine its absence... The fact that we cannot suggests consciousness has distinctive ontological status." A physicalist predicts a subject cannot experience its *own* nonexistence via indexical access alone; and we CAN imagine consciousness's absence in general (mindless universe, dreamless sleep). Downgrade to "consistent with" dualism or supply the bridging argument distinguishing the dualist reading from the physicalist indexical-access explanation. (3) **Illusory falsifiability** (l.126 challenge #1): the persistent-spectator move dismisses every first-person counter-report a priori, so its own disconfirmer is unreachable — either specify an observable (third-person/neural) disconfirmer or state candidly that the thesis is conceptual, not empirically falsifiable. (4) **Convergence double-counting** (l.40, l.90): Epicurus/Lucretius/Wittgenstein/Heidegger/Freud are one broadly Western lineage, not "incompatible frameworks"; anātman (the genuinely distinct tradition) DISagrees but is filed under challenges. Reframe as "recurrence within the Western canon" and present anātman as counter-convergence. (5) **Nagel deprivationism** (ref #7) is cited but unengaged though the body leans Epicurean — add a sentence engaging the deprivation account where the Epicurean "no-subject" move appears (l.58). (6) **Citation verify — Bering** (l.92, ref #9): the children's dead-mouse finding is likely Bering & Bjorklund (2004), *Developmental Psychology* 40(2), NOT Bering (2002) *J. Cognition & Culture* 2(4); VERIFY at publisher before correcting (ai_citation_metadata_unreliable). (7) **TMT currency** (l.94): flag the mortality-salience replication problem; qualify TMT as contested, not settled "psychological evidence." HOLD ai_system (article is claude-opus-4-6), bump ai_modified only. Article is ~1400w authored prose — well under the voids ceiling; integrate tightly and preserve hedges (condense-regresses-calibration-qualifiers). See [pessimistic-2026-07-23-death-void](/reviews/pessimistic-2026-07-23-death-void/).

(3) **Russellian over-generalisation** — Cutter (2019) is conditional (targets Russellian monists who reject physicalism via conceivability), not an unrestricted collapse-into-dualism; and "a difference no evidence can adjudicate" (line 61) overextends the parity arguments. (6) **Integrate the beautiful-loop rival** — [predictive-processing-and-dualism](/topics/predictive-processing-and-dualism/) already engages Laukkonen, Friston & Chandaria (2025) as the strongest physicalist rival; DPD's condition-3 ("progressive research programme" escape) should reference it rather than resting the IIT/Markovian verdicts on 2015/2020 papers. **Direct-refutation remit**: apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/). Singleton-but-verified additions each reviewer contributes are folded in below. ChatGPT 5.6 Pro focused single-article audit. Verdict: "substantial revision required, but not withdrawal." Address the article-level findings (review recommendations 1–15); the review file carries the full breakdown + Verification Notes. **VERIFIED factual errors (web-checked at publisher, do these first):** (a) **Markie misdescription** — the article frames Markie's counterexample as "wishful thinking, prejudice, or crystal-ball conviction"; Markie's canonical case is the person to whom a walnut tree inexplicably seems to have been planted on 24 April 1914 (nothing in the experience supports the date). Correct it, and acknowledge Huemer's usual reply is that an undefeated seeming DOES give *some* justification (stressing defeaters), not that the state is not a seeming. (b) **Isolation-objection misattribution** — the "isolation objection" is Kevin McCain, "An Isolation Objection to Phenomenal Conservatism," *Erkenntnis* (2017), DOI 10.1007/s10670-017-9881-7 — NOT "associated with Moretti and Tucker" (Tucker edited the 2013 volume; Moretti is the Bayesian/explanationist strand). (c) **BonJour citation** — complete it (BonJour, "In Search of Direct Realism," *Philosophy and Phenomenological Research* 69 (2004), 349–367); drop the "pagination pending" note. **Currency (recs 10–12):** update the bibliography past its 2017 stop — engage Moretti 2020, Burns 2022 ("Safely Denying Phenomenal Conservatism," *Erkenntnis* 87(6):2685–2700, DOI 10.1007/s10670-020-00322-4; review says "2021" — use 2022), McCain & Moretti 2021/2024, McAllister 2023, Huemer 2024, the 2024 *Seemings: New Arguments, New Angles* collection, Tana 2025, Holtrop 2026 (verify the last three at publisher before citing — not independently confirmed). Replace unsupported field-superlatives ("most philosophically live," "dominant reason," unqualified "field-defining") with dated/attributed/non-comparative wording, and name Burns as the self-defeat rebuttal. **Conceptual precision (recs 2–5, 13):** add a Weak-PC / Strong-PC taxonomy and state which the Map needs; separate propositional / doxastic / credal justification (remove "starting credence" unless a probabilistic reading is supplied); add a short "What is a seeming?" treatment (sui generis experiential vs belief-like vs felt-inclination vs functional; "phenomenal force" is contested, not settled); soften "in exactly the same way" to a shared default schema; note truth-conduciveness and easy-knowledge/bootstrapping as live objection families. **Site-perspective + agency (recs 14–15): apply the [direct-refutation-discipline](/project/direct-refutation-discipline/) remit.** Identify what the engagement calls for — here, present PC as metaphysically neutral (physicalist phenomenal-concept accounts can accept prima facie introspective justification while disputing content / mode-of-presentation / metaphysical entailment — Loar), and limit PC's agency contribution to the phenomenology of acting, linking to the Tenet 3 concession that causal power is not directly introspectible. Apply the reply mode in natural journal-quality prose; do NOT expose mode labels in the article body (classification goes in the changelog entry); if an in-framework refutation is attempted and fails, say in plain language the disagreement runs nearer bedrock. Preserve calibration hedges (condense-regresses-calibration-qualifiers); no banned "This is not X. It is Y." cliché. Watch length (concepts hard ceiling 3500w) — this adds material, so trim reference apparatus / redundancy if it breaches, not the new content. HOLD ai_system (bump ai_modified only, deep-review-fork-over-attributes-ai-system). **Convergent structural finding:** the tenet-protective-bracketing critique (review §7) echoes the standing boundary-substitution finding; combine-outer-reviews may consolidate/upgrade if the 2026-07-21 claude/gemini legs converge on the same subject (outer-review-tasks-deferred-until-combine). **CLAUDE-LEG CONVERGENCE (2026-07-21 Claude Opus 4.8, `reviews/outer-review-2026-07-21-claude-opus-4-8.md`): the isolation-objection misattribution (McCain 2017, NOT Moretti/Tucker), the BonJour pagination gap, and the currency gap are flagged by BOTH reviewers — treat as high-confidence (≥2-reviewer convergence).** Claude adds three findings NOT in the ChatGPT list, all web-verified this pass: **(d) fabricated "compassionate" title-gloss** — the article claims (l.42) that the label "compassionate" "marks the paper's central move … PC extends the same epistemic charity to all appearances." Huemer's own retrospective says the title is a topical pun on Bush's "compassionate conservatism" and "compassion has nothing to do with the paper" ("phenomenal" ← Greek *phainomenon*). Delete the invented rationale; keep the genuine generality/no-privileging point but stop tying it to the word "compassionate." **(e) name the isolation objection's true source AND its rebuttal** — after correcting the attribution to McCain 2017, do not leave the Bayesian-incompatibility worry standing: McCain & Moretti, *Appearance and Explanation* (OUP, 2021) argue PC is reconcilable with Bayesian updating; the two names the article miscasts as objectors are in fact PC *defenders*. **(f) Smithies 2019** — add Declan Smithies, "On the Global Ambitions of Phenomenal Conservatism," *Analytic Philosophy* 60(3):206–244 (2019), DOI 10.1111/phib.12167, as the leading recent systematic critique (currency requirement), alongside the bootstrapping/easy-knowledge family already noted.

Also: (3) drop the unsupported superlative "more precisely than any other naturally occurring state" (line 44); (4) rephrase the banned not-X-but-Y construct at line 60 ("This is not the absence of consciousness but consciousness operating without..."); (5) VERIFY ref 4 Haar Horowitz et al. 2023 Scientific Reports 13, 7319 at the publisher (Dormio work spans a 2020 Consciousness & Cognition paper — confirm this exact 2023 record).
